# UX issues

The UX loop maintains this file (`/ux`). There is one line per issue, and the line moves to the section for its current status.

- **Line format:** ID · severity (S1–S4; P0–P2 for UX_REVIEW items) · area · status · title · main `path:line` · the run that last changed it, then a short note.
- **Areas:**
  - A — voice capture;
  - B — calendar, home and tasks;
  - C — lists, notes, groups and sharing;
  - D — first run, sign-in, settings, notifications, native surfaces and theme.
- **Notes:** P# and D# are the packages and decisions in that run's `plan.md`. "Merged" lists the duplicates folded into a line.

Partly fixed in b7 round 3 (P97; reviewer: partly, failed 1 time): `taskRepositoryProvider` and `captureOwnerProvider` now watch only `account?.id`, so an account update no longer turns Task details into a spinner or runs a second sync (tests: `account_update_test.dart`). Still failing: after every sync that ends with the task open, the scroll position moves; `account_update_test.dart:381-396` ("stays where the user scrolled") fails. Cause, outside P97's files: `attachmentEntriesProvider` (`attachment_panel.dart:25-29`) watches `taskProvider`, and `attachments.when(...)` at :218 (default `skipLoadingOnReload: false`) swaps the file cards for a 4 dp progress bar on each reload, so a user at the bottom of a long task is pushed up by about one card per file. Reviewer's fix: pass `skipLoadingOnReload: true` at :218; `_busy`'s bar at :208 already shows the user's own progress; no copy change. Not re-planned: b7 ended after round 3. Phone: on a free account, open a long task, scroll to the bottom, attach a photo; the card never disappears and the view doesn't move Fixed in code 2026-10-14 by the main session, after b7, with the reviewer's one-line fix: `skipLoadingOnReload: true` on `attachments.when(...)` in `attachment_panel.dart`. The failing test `account_update_test.dart` ('stays where the user scrolled') now passes. Not re-verified by a reviewer. Phone: on a free account, open a long task, scroll to the bottom and attach a photo; the card never disappears and the view stays put.

Still open, cause server-side: `Backend/core/serializers.py` (~:295) and `Backend/core/ai.py:88-112` don't use it yet, for the main session. Phone, after the server half: Voz on Català, `En línia, en acabar`, Catalan words come back Server half done 2026-10-14 by the main session (520742c): the language reaches the transcription model as a hint, and a refused hint falls back to transcribing without it. Accepted side effect: a fresh dictation whose only date has passed («ayer») keeps the words in the title with no day. Tests: `App/test/capture_free_dictations_test.dart:3077`–`:3212` (five cases). Phone: on the emulator lane, share, kill the app, move the date forward, resume from Sin guardar

- **UX-912** · S2 · A · wontfix · Plain dictation's wait with commands on is unmeasured · `App/lib/services/capture_processor.dart:229` · 2026-10-18-commands · D11, with UX-616: every dictation now asks for commands. Measure on staging (main session), same phone and network: 10+ one-task dictations with the flag and 10+ without, `extraction_ms` p50 and p95 in Diagnóstico de captura. If the flag adds 300 ms or more at p50, the cause is server-side (a longer prompt): decide it with UX-616. Inferred, not measured. Decided (D11, 2026-10-18 night): measured locally on 2026-10-18 instead of on staging, with the real free model (`gemma-4-26b-a4b-it`), 12 one-task Spanish dictations each way, server extraction time only: median 3.5 s with the commands flag, 4.3 s without (p95 3.8 s and 4.6 s), one task each and no false commands. The flag stays; no change
- **UX-616** · S2 · A · wontfix · The assistant's wait (about 5 s) is capture's longest · `App/lib/services/capture_processor.dart:207` · 2026-10-18-commands · review A's question 2: cut it on the server, with a faster model or by understanding while the person speaks? Staging: 1.3, 4.8, 4.8, 4.8 and 20.5 s. A cost decision; the app's share is UX-610 (P101). Severity set by the organizer. 2026-10-18-commands: UX-912 (D11) asks to measure, with it, what asking for commands adds to every dictation's wait. Decided 2026-10-18 (night) for the owner; cause server-side: settings only, `AI_ROUTE=auto` with `AI_FREE_ATTEMPT_SECONDS=10` on staging and production, so a slow free call falls back to the paid model sooner; it applies at the next deploy, which stays the owner's. D11 (UX-912) found the commands flag adds no wait. The app's share stays UX-610 (device check)
